“You are cunning, sir, but I remember what Freydis told me. Will you swear that Misery cannot bring back the dead?”

“Very willingly I will swear to it, upon all the most authentic relics in Christendom.”

“Ah, yes, but will you rest one of your cold hard pointed ears against”﻿—here Manuel whispered what he did not care to name aloud﻿—“the while that you swear to it.”

===Chinese===
曼努埃尔大人说：「我讨那个妮亚菲尔，她做过女奴，如今是她那异教徒乐园里的一缕幽魂。」

「那么，你以为死者是召得回来的么？」

「你够机灵，先生，可我记得弗蕾迪丝对我说过的话。你肯起誓，说苦难不能起死回生么？」

「我十二分乐意起这个誓，凭基督教世界里一切最可靠的圣物起誓。」

「啊，是了，可是你肯不肯把你那只又冷又硬的尖耳朵贴在」——说到这里，曼努埃尔悄声说出了一件他不肯放声讲出的东西——「上头，一面就这样起誓么？」

===Original===
“Of course not,” Misery answered, sullenly: “since every troubled ghost that ever gibbered and clanked chains would rise confronting me if I made such an oath. Yes, Manuel, I am able to bring back the dead, but prudence forces me to lie about my power, because to exercise that power to the full would be well-nigh as ruinous as the breaking of that pumpkin. For there is only one way to bring back the dead in flesh, and if I follow that way I shall lose my head as all the others have done.”

“What is that to a lover?” says Manuel.

The head sighed, and bit at its white lips. “An oath is an oath to the Léshy. Therefore do you, who are human, now make profitable use of the knowledge and of the power you get from those other women by breaking oaths! And as you have served me, so will I serve you.”

===Chinese===
「当然不肯，」苦难怏怏地答道，「因为一旦我立下这样的誓，凡是曾经呓语哀号、拖得锁链哗啷作响的冤魂，个个都会升起来同我当面对质。是的，曼努埃尔，我是起得回死者的，只是谨慎逼得我在自己的法力上说谎，因为把这份法力使到十足，差不多会同砸破那只南瓜一样招灾。须知叫死者血肉还阳，路子只有一条，而我若走了那条路子，便会同先前所有走过的人一样，丢掉我自己的头。」

「这在一个情人又算得了什么？」曼努埃尔说道。

那颗头叹了口气，咬住它那两片白嘴唇。「对莱西来说，誓约便是誓约。所以你——你可是个人——如今正好去把从那另外几位女人那里得来的学问和法力，用背弃誓约的手段，派上划算的用场！你怎样侍奉了我，我便怎样侍奉你。」

===Original===
Manuel called black eagles to him, in the manner the Princess Alianora had taught, and he sent them into all parts of the world for every sort of white earth. They obeyed the magic of the Apsarasas, and from Britain they brought Dom Manuel the earth called leucargillon, and they brought glisomarga from Enisgarth, and eglecopala from the Gallic provinces, and argentaria from Lacre Kai, and white earth of every description from all parts of the world.

Manuel made from this earth, as Queen Freydis had taught him how to do, the body of a woman. He fashioned the body peculiarly, in accordance with the old Tuyla mystery, and the body was as perfect as Manuel could make it, in all ways save that it had no head.

Then Manuel sent a gold-crested wren into Provence: it entered through an upper window of the King’s marmoreal palace, and went into the Princess Alianora’s chamber, and fetched hence a handkerchief figured with yellow mulberries and wet with the tears which Alianora had shed in her grieving for Manuel. And Dom Manuel sent also a falcon, which returned to him with Queen Freydis’ handkerchief. That was figured with white fleurs-de-lis, and that too was drenched with tears.

===Chinese===
曼努埃尔照阿莉亚诺拉公主传授的法子唤来黑鹰，差它们飞往世界各地，去取各式各样的白土。它们服从阿普萨拉斯的魔法，从不列颠给曼努埃尔大人衔来了那名为留卡吉隆（leucargillon）的土，又从埃尼斯加斯（Enisgarth）衔来格利索玛加（glisomarga），从高卢诸省衔来埃格勒科帕拉（eglecopala），从拉克雷凯（Lacre Kai）衔来阿尔真塔里亚（argentaria），还有世界各地形形色色的白土。

曼努埃尔照弗蕾迪丝女王传授的法子，用这些土捏成一个女人的身体。他遵照古老的图伊拉秘法，把身体造得别有讲究；这身体各方面都尽曼努埃尔之所能做到尽善尽美，唯独没有头。

随后曼努埃尔把一只金冠戴菊（gold-crested wren）遣往普罗旺斯：它从国王那座大理石宫殿楼上的一扇窗子飞了进去，进到阿莉亚诺拉公主的闺房，从那里衔出一方手帕——帕上绣着黄桑葚的图案，浸透了阿莉亚诺拉哀念曼努埃尔时洒下的眼泪。曼努埃尔大人还放出一只猎鹰，猎鹰带着弗蕾迪丝女王的手帕回到他跟前。那方手帕绣的是白色鸢尾花饰（fleurs-de-lis），也同样叫泪水浸得透湿。

===Original===
Whereupon, all being in readiness, Misery smiled craftily, and said:

“In the time that is passed I have overthrown high kings and prophets, and sorcerers also, as when Misery half carelessly made sport of Mithridates and of Merlin and of Moses, in ways that ballad-singers still delight to tell of. But with you, Dom Manuel, I shall deal otherwise, and I shall disconcert you by and by in a more quiet fashion. Hoh, I must grapple carefully with your love for Niafer, as with an antagonist who is not scrupulous, nor very sensible, but who is exceedingly strong. For observe: you obstinately desire this perished heathen woman, who in life, it well may be, was nothing remarkable. Therefore you have sought Misery, you have dwelt for a month of years with terror, you have surrendered youth, you are planning to defy death, you are intent to rob the deep grave and to despoil paradise. Truly your love is great.”

===Chinese===
于是，一切齐备，苦难狡黠地一笑，说道：

「往昔之时，我掀翻过列位尊高的国王和先知，也掀翻过术士——就像当年苦难半不经意地拿米特里达梯（Mithridates）、梅林和摩西（Moses）打趣那样，那些手法，谣曲歌手们至今还爱传唱。可对你，曼努埃尔大人，我要另换一副手段，日后自会用一种更安静的法子教你不自在。嚯，你这份对妮亚菲尔的爱，我得当心着同它角力，就同对付一个对手一样：这对手不讲什么道义，也说不上怎么明白事理，力气却大得惊人。因为你看：你执迷地恋慕这个亡故的异教女人，而她在世的时候，很可能根本算不得什么出奇人物。于是你来寻访苦难，你伴着恐怖度过了岁月之月，你交出了青春，你盘算着蔑视死亡，你一心要洗劫幽深的坟墓、掳掠乐园。你的爱当真伟大。」

===Original===
Manuel said only, “An obligation is upon me, for the life of Niafer was given to preserve my life.”

“Now I, whom some call Béda, and others Kruchina, and whom for the present your love has conquered﻿—I it is, alone, who can obtain for you this woman, because in the long run I overcome all things and persons. Life is my province, and the birth cry of every infant is an oath of allegiance to me. Thus I am overlord where all serve willy-nilly except you, who have served of your own will. And as you have served me, so must I serve you.”

Manuel said, “That is well.”

===Chinese===
曼努埃尔只说：「我身上负着一道义务，因为妮亚菲尔的性命，是为保全我的性命而交出去的。」

「如今我——有人称我贝达，也有人叫我克鲁奇娜，此刻又被你的爱征服了的——偏偏是我，独自一人能为你弄到这个女人，因为归根到底，一切事物、一切人等，终都要被我压倒。生命是我的封疆，每一个婴儿坠地的啼哭，都是向我效忠的誓词。于是我便是宗主：在我的治下，人人都身不由己地服侍我，只有你例外，你是出于自愿侍奉我的。你怎样侍奉了我，我就必须怎样侍奉你。」

曼努埃尔说：「那很好。」

===Original===
“It is not so well as you think, for when you have this Niafer I shall return to you in the appearance of a light formless cloud, and I shall rise about you, not suddenly but a little by a little. So shall you see through me the woman for love of whom your living was once made high-hearted and fearless, and for whose sake death was derided, and paradise was ransacked: and you will ask forlornly, ‘Was it for this?’ Throughout the orderly, busied, unimportant hours that stretch between your dressing for the day and your undressing for the night, you will be asking this question secretly in your heart, while I pass everywhither with you in the appearance of a light formless cloud, and whisper to you secretly.”

“And what will you whisper to me?”

“Not anything which you will care to repeat to anybody anywhere. Oh, you will be able to endure it, and you will be content, as human contentment goes, and my triumph will not be public. But, none the less, I shall have overthrown my present conqueror, and I shall have brought low the love which terror and death did not affright, and which the laws of earth could not control; and I, whom some call Béda, and others Kruchina, will very terribly attest that the ghost of outlived and conquered misery is common-sense.”

===Chinese===
「这可不像你想的那么好。因为等你得到了这个妮亚菲尔，我会化作一朵轻而无形的云气回到你身边，在你四围升腾——不骤然，而是一点一点的。于是你将透过我，去看那个女人：为了对她的爱，你在世为人一度心气高扬、无所畏惧；为了她的缘故，死亡曾遭讥诮，乐园曾遭洗劫：而你会凄凄然问道：『就为的是这个？』从你清早穿衣到夜里脱衣之间，那一连串有条不紊、忙忙叨叨、无关紧要的时辰里，你会在心里暗暗盘问这个问题，而我化作一朵轻而无形的云气，随你游遍四方，向你窃窃私语。」

「那你要向我窃窃私语些什么呢？」

「无非是些你在任何地方都不愿意讲给任何人听的话。哦，你受得住的，你也会知足——照人间的知足来说——我的凯旋也不会张扬于外。可是不管怎样，我终究掀翻了我眼下的征服者，终究贬抑了那股恐怖与死亡都吓不退、尘世的律法也拘束不住的爱；而我——有人称我贝达，也有人叫我克鲁奇娜——将极其可怕地作证：已被活过、已被克服的苦难的幽魂，便是常识。」

===Original===
“That is tomorrow’s affair,” replied Dom Manuel “Today there is an obligation upon me, and my dealings are with today.”

Then Manuel bound the clay head of Misery in the two handkerchiefs which were wet with the tears of Alianora and of Freydis. When the cock had crowed three times, Dom Manuel unbound the head, and it was only a shapeless mass of white clay, because of the tears of Freydis and Alianora.

Manuel modeled in this clay, to the best of his ability, the head of Niafer, as he remembered her when they had loved each other upon Vraidex: and after the white head was finished he fitted it to the body which he had made from the other kinds of white earth. Dom Manuel robed this body in brown drugget such as Niafer had been used to wear in and about the kitchen at Arnaye, and he did the other things that were requisite, for this was the day of All Saints when nothing sacred ought to be neglected.

===Chinese===
「那是明天的事，」曼努埃尔大人答道，「今天我身上负着一道义务，我打交道的对象是今天。」

于是曼努埃尔用那两方浸透阿莉亚诺拉与弗蕾迪丝泪水的手帕，把苦难的泥头颅包裹起来。等公鸡叫过三遍，曼努埃尔大人解开手帕，那颗头已经只是一团不成形状的白黏土了——全因为弗蕾迪丝和阿莉亚诺拉的眼泪。

曼努埃尔凭着力所能及，用这黏土捏出妮亚菲尔的头，照他记忆中两人在弗赖德克斯彼此相爱时她的模样：白头捏成之后，他把它装配到那具用其余几种白土捏成的身体上。曼努埃尔大人给这身体穿上棕色粗呢的衣裙，正是妮亚菲尔从前在阿尔奈厨房里里外外惯穿的那一种；其余该做的事他也一一做了，因为这一天是万圣节，凡神圣的事都不容疏忽。

## XIII What Helmas Directed / 十三 黑尔马斯的指点

===Original===
Now the Count of Poictesme departs from Provence, with his lackeys carrying his images, and early in April he comes to Helmas the Deep-Minded. The wise King was then playing with his small daughter Mélusine (who later dethroned and imprisoned him), but he sent the child away with a kiss, and he attentively heard Dom Manuel through.

King Helmas looked at the images, prodded them with a shriveled forefinger, and cleared his throat; and then said nothing, because, after all, Dom Manuel was Count of Poictesme.

“What is needed?” said Manuel.

“They are not true to life,” replied Helmas﻿—“particularly this one which has the look of me.”

===Chinese===
如今，波伊捷斯伯爵（Count of Poictesme）离开了普罗旺斯（Provence），仆从们替他抬着那几尊泥像；四月初，他来到深谋的黑尔马斯（Helmas the Deep-Minded）跟前。那位贤明的国王当时正陪着他年幼的女儿美露莘（Mélusine）（正是她后来废黜了他、又囚禁了他）玩耍，但他吻了吻孩子，把她打发开去，然后凝神专意，听曼努埃尔大人（Dom Manuel）把话讲完。

黑尔马斯国王端详着那些泥像，用干瘪的食指戳了戳，清了清嗓子；随后却什么也没说，因为曼努埃尔大人毕竟已是波伊捷斯伯爵。

「还需要什么？」曼努埃尔说。

「它们不逼真，」黑尔马斯答道——「尤其是这一尊，瞧着就是我的模样。」

===Original===
“Yes, I know that: but who can give life to my images?”

King Helmas pushed back his second best crown, wherein was set the feather from the wing of the miller’s goose, and he scratched his forehead. He said, “There is a power over all figures of earth and a queen whose will is neither to loose nor to bind.” Helmas turned toward a thick book, wherein was magic.

“Yes, *queen* is the same as *cwen*. Therefore Queen Freydis of Audela might help you.”

“Yes, for it is she that owns Schamir. But the falcons are not nesting now, and how can I go to Freydis, that woman of strange deeds?”

===Chinese===
「是，这我知道：可谁能赋与我的泥像生命呢？」

黑尔马斯国王把他那第二好的王冠往后一推——冠上缀着磨坊主家那只鹅翅上的羽毛——又搔了搔额头。他说：「大地上的一切形象之上，另有一股主宰之力；还有一位女王，她的意志既无可解、亦无可缚。」黑尔马斯转身翻起一部厚厚的书，书里载着魔法。

「不错，*queen* 与 *cwen* 本是同一个词。所以奥德拉（Audela）的弗蕾迪丝女王（Freydis）兴许能帮你。」

「是，沙米尔（Schamir）正是在她手里。可如今隼儿还不筑巢，我又怎能上弗蕾迪丝那儿去——那个行事离奇的女人？」

===Original===
“Oh, people nowadays no longer use falcons; and of course nobody can go to Freydis uninvited. Still, it can be managed that Freydis will come to you when the moon is void and powerless, and when this and that has been arranged.”

Thereafter Helmas the Deep-Minded told Count Manuel what was requisite. “So you will need such and such things,” says King Helmas, “but, above all, do not forget the ointment.”

Count Manuel went alone into Poictesme, which was his fief if only he could get it. He came secretly to Upper Morven, that place of horrible fame. Near the ten-colored stone, whereon men had sacrificed to Vel-Tyno in time’s youth, he builded an enclosure of peeled willow wands, and spread butter upon them, and tied them with knots of yellow ribbons, as Helmas had directed. Manuel arranged all matters within the enclosure as Helmas had directed. There Manuel waited, on the last night in April, regarding the full moon.

In a while you saw the shadowings on the moon’s radiancy begin to waver and move: later they passed from the moon’s face like little clouds, and the moon was naked of markings. This was a token that the Moon-Children had gone to the well from which once a month they fetch water, and that for an hour the moon would be void and powerless. With this and that ceremony Count Manuel kindled such a fire upon the old altar of Vel-Tyno as Helmas had directed.

===Chinese===
「哦，如今人们用隼的不多了；当然啰，不经邀请，谁也上弗蕾迪丝那儿去不了。不过，自有办法叫弗蕾迪丝在月亮空虚无力的时辰来到你跟前，只消这样那样的事情都布置妥帖。」

此后，深谋的黑尔马斯便把必不可少的门道一一指点给曼努埃尔伯爵。「这样，你就需要如此这般的东西，」黑尔马斯国王说，「但顶要紧的是，千万别忘了那药膏。」

曼努埃尔伯爵孤身进了波伊捷斯——那是他的封地，只消他拿得到手。他悄悄来到上莫尔文（Upper Morven），那声名可怖的地方。在十色之石近旁——在时光的幼年，人们曾在那石上向维尔-蒂诺（Vel-Tyno）献祭——他用剥了皮的柳条扎起一道围场，在柳条上涂抹黄油，又用黄丝带打的结束缚起来，一切照黑尔马斯的指点办理。围场之内的种种事项，曼努埃尔也照黑尔马斯的指点一一布置停当。四月最末的一夜，曼努埃尔便在那里守候，凝望着满月。

过不多时，你便看见月亮光华上的阴影开始摇曳、移动：随后它们像一朵朵小云似的从月亮脸上掠过，月亮变得光洁无痕。这是一个凭证：月中孩童（Moon-Children）已经到那口井去了——每月一回，他们从那井里汲水——在这一个时辰里，月亮便要空虚无力的了。曼努埃尔伯爵行了这样那样的仪式，照黑尔马斯的指点，在维尔-蒂诺的古老祭坛上燃起了火。

===Original===
Manuel cried aloud: “Now be propitious, infernal, terrestrial and celestial Bombo! Lady of highways, patroness of crossroads, thou who bearest the light! Thou who dost labor always in obscurity, thou enemy of the day, thou friend and companion of darkness! Thou rejoicing in the barking of dogs and in shed blood, thus do I honor thee.”

Manuel did as Helmas had directed, and for an instant the screamings were pitiable, but the fire ended these speedily.

Then Manuel cried, again: “O thou who wanderest amid shadows and over tombs, and dost tether even the strong sea! O whimsical sister of the blighting sun, and fickle mistress of old death! O Gorgo, Mormo, lady of a thousand forms and qualities! now view with a propitious eye my sacrifice!”

===Chinese===
曼努埃尔高声喊道：「愿冥间的、地上的和天上的邦波（Bombo）垂怜于我！道路的夫人，十字路口的主保，擎着灯火的你！永远在幽暗里操劳的你，白昼的仇敌，黑暗的朋友与伴侣！以犬吠和洒血为乐的你啊——我如此敬奉你。」

曼努埃尔照黑尔马斯的指点做了；有一瞬间，尖叫声凄惨可悯，但火焰很快便把它们了结了。

随后曼努埃尔又喊道：「哦，你游走于阴影之间、坟墓之上，连浩荡的大海也拴得住！哦，摧残万物的烈日那任性的姐妹，古老死亡那善变的情妇！哦，戈耳戈（Gorgo），摩尔莫（Mormo），身具千种形貌、千般品格的夫人！请垂下怜眼，看看我的祭品！」

===Original===
Thus Manuel spoke, and steadily the fire upon the altar grew larger and brighter as he nourished it repugnantly.

When the fire was the height of a warrior, and queer things were happening to this side and to that side, Count Manuel spoke the ordered words: and of a sudden the flames’ colors were altered, so that green shimmerings showed in the fire, as though salt were burning there. Manuel waited. This greenness shifted and writhed and increased in the heart of the fire, and out of the fire oozed a green serpent, the body of which was well-nigh as thick as a man’s body.

This portent came toward Count Manuel horribly. He, who was familiar with serpents, now grasped this monster’s throat, and to the touch its scales were like very cold glass.

===Chinese===
曼努埃尔这样念诵着，一面满心厌恶地喂养着那火，祭坛上的火便渐渐旺起来、亮起来。

等火焰窜到一个战士的高度，两边都有怪事发生，曼努埃尔伯爵便念出了那依吩咐备下的字句：霎时间，火焰的颜色变了，火中泛出点点绿光，仿佛有盐在里头烧着。曼努埃尔等待着。这绿色在火心里挪移、扭动、越涨越大，从火里渗出一条绿蛇来，那身子几乎有人的身子一般粗。

这怪物骇人地朝曼努埃尔伯爵逼来。他本是同蛇打惯了交道的，当下便一把攥住这怪物的喉咙——触手之处，它的鳞片竟像极冷的玻璃。

===Original===
The great snake shifted so resistlessly that Manuel was forced back toward the fire and toward a doom more dreadful than burning: and the firelight was in the snake’s contemptuous wise eyes. Manuel was of stalwart person, but his strength availed him nothing until he began to recite aloud, as Helmas had directed, the multiplication tables: Freydis could not withstand mathematics.

So when Manuel had come to two times eleven the tall fire guttered as though it bended under the passing of a strong wind: then the flames burned high, and Manuel could see that he was grasping the throat of a monstrous pig. He, who was familiar with pigs, could see that this was a black pig, caked with dried curds of the Milky Way; its flesh was chill to the touch, like dead flesh; and it had long tusks, which possessed life of their own, and groped and writhed toward Manuel like fat white worms.

Then Manuel said, as Helmas had directed: “Solomon’s provision for one day was thirty measures of fine flour, and threescore measures of meal, ten fat oxen, and twenty oxen out of the pastures, and a hundred sheep, beside harts, and roebucks, and fallow deer, and fatted fowl. But Elijah the Tishbite was fed by ravens that brought him bread and flesh.”

Again the tall flames guttered. Now Manuel was grasping a thick heatless slab of crystal, like a mirror, wherein he could see himself quite clearly. Just as he really was, he, who was not familiar with such mirrors, could see Count Manuel, housed in a little wet dirt with old inveterate stars adrift about him everywhither; and the spectacle was enough to frighten anybody.

===Chinese===
大蛇挪动起来，那力气简直不可抗拒，曼努埃尔被逼得直朝火退去，退向一种比焚烧更可怕的厄运：火光正映在那蛇轻蔑而狡慧的眼睛里。曼努埃尔身躯魁伟，可他的力气全不顶用，直到他照黑尔马斯的指点，开始高声背诵乘法表——弗蕾迪丝敌不过数学。

于是，等曼努埃尔背到二乘十一，那高高的火苗便一阵摇曳，仿佛被一阵劲风压得弯了腰：随后火焰重又高高烧起，曼努埃尔看清了，自己攥着的，是一头巨猪的喉咙。他本是同猪打惯了交道的，看得出这是一头黑猪，周身结着干涸的银河凝乳；它的皮肉触手冰凉，像死肉一般；它还长着两根长牙，那牙自有其生命，像肥白的大蛆一般，朝曼努埃尔摸索过来、扭动过来。

随后曼努埃尔照黑尔马斯的指点说道：「所罗门（Solomon）一日的口粮，是细面三十份，粗面六十份，肥牛十头，牧场上的牛二十头，羊一百只，此外还有公鹿、狍子、扁角鹿和肥禽。可提斯比人以利亚（Elijah the Tishbite），却是乌鸦养活的，它们给他叼来饼和肉。」

高高的火焰重又一阵摇曳。此刻曼努埃尔攥着的，是一块厚厚的、没有热气的晶体，像一面镜子，他在里面能把自个儿看得清清楚楚。他——一个不惯照这种镜子的人——看见曼努埃尔伯爵的本来面目：栖身在一小片湿漉漉的泥土上，四面八方漂着古老的、积习难改的群星；这景象，足够吓坏随便哪一个人。

===Original===
So Manuel said: “The elephant is the largest of all animals, and in intelligence approaches the nearest to man. Its nostril is elongated, and answers to the purpose of a hand. Its toes are undivided, and it lives two hundred years. Africa breeds elephants, but India produces the largest.”

The mirror now had melted into a dark warm fluid which oozed between his fingers, dripping to the ground. But Manuel held tightly to what remained between his palms, and he felt, they say, that in the fluid was struggling something small and soft and living, as though he held a tiny minnow.

Said Manuel, “A straight line is the shortest distance between two points.”

Of a sudden the fire became an ordinary fire, and the witches of Amneran screamed, and Morven was emptied of sorcery, and Count Manuel was grasping the warm soft throat of a woman. Instantly he had her within the enclosure of peeled willow wands that had been spread with butter and tied with knots of yellow ribbon, because into such an enclosure the power and the dominion of Freydis could never enter.

All these things Manuel did precisely as King Helmas had directed.

===Chinese===
于是曼努埃尔说：「象是所有动物里最大的，论智力也最接近人。它的鼻子里延得很长，抵得上一只手使唤。它的脚趾不分瓣，能活二百年。非洲产象，可印度产的象最大。」

这时那面镜子已经化成一泓深色的暖流，从他指缝间渗出，滴落在地上。但曼努埃尔紧紧攥住双掌间剩下的东西，而且，据说，他觉出那流体里有个小小的、柔软的、活着的东西在挣扎，仿佛他捧着的是一尾小小的鲦鱼。

曼努埃尔说：「两点之间，直线最短。」

霎时间，那火变成了一堆平平常常的火，阿姆内兰（Amneran）的女巫们尖叫起来，莫尔文（Morven）上的妖法为之一空，曼努埃尔伯爵攥住的，是一个女人温软的喉咙。他立时把她拢进了那道剥了皮的柳条围场——涂了黄油、用黄丝带打结束缚的那道——因为弗蕾迪丝的权能与统辖，永远进不了这样一道围场。

这一切，曼努埃尔都一丝不差地照着黑尔马斯国王的指点做了。
